      SELECT [i].[Id], [i].[Barcode], [i].[CategoryId], [i].[Code], [i].[CostPrice], [i].[CreatedAt], [i].[CreatedBy], [i].[CurrentStock], [i].[Description], [i].[IsActive], [i].[IsDeleted], [i].[IsSystem], [i].[MaxStockLevel], [i].[MinStockLevel], [i].[NameAr], [i].[NameEn], [i].[SalePrice], [i].[UnitId], [i].[UpdatedAt], [i].[UpdatedBy], [c0].[Id], [c0].[Code], [c0].[CreatedAt], [c0].[CreatedBy], [c0].[Description], [c0].[IsActive], [c0].[IsDeleted], [c0].[IsSystem], [c0].[NameAr], [c0].[NameEn], [c0].[ParentCategoryId], [c0].[UpdatedAt], [c0].[UpdatedBy], [u0].[Id], [u0].[Code], [u0].[CreatedAt], [u0].[CreatedBy], [u0].[IsActive], [u0].[IsDeleted], [u0].[IsSystem], [u0].[NameAr], [u0].[NameEn], [u0].[UpdatedAt], [u0].[UpdatedBy]
      FROM [Items] AS [i]
      INNER JOIN (
          SELECT [c].[Id], [c].[Code], [c].[CreatedAt], [c].[CreatedBy], [c].[Description], [c].[IsActive], [c].[IsDeleted], [c].[IsSystem], [c].[NameAr], [c].[NameEn], [c].[ParentCategoryId], [c].[UpdatedAt], [c].[UpdatedBy]
          FROM [Categories] AS [c]
          WHERE [c].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
      ) AS [c0] ON [i].[CategoryId] = [c0].[Id]
      INNER JOIN (
          SELECT [u].[Id], [u].[Code], [u].[CreatedAt], [u].[CreatedBy], [u].[IsActive], [u].[IsDeleted], [u].[IsSystem], [u].[NameAr], [u].[NameEn], [u].[UpdatedAt], [u].[UpdatedBy]
          FROM [Units] AS [u]
          WHERE [u].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
      ) AS [u0] ON [i].[UnitId] = [u0].[Id]
      WHERE [i].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
      ORDER BY [i].[Code]
